- **hig-guide**: Apple's Human Interface Guidelines, put to work: review UI code or designs against what Apple actually says, choose the right control or presentation (sheet, popover, alert or full screen; tab bar, sidebar or split view), look up exact figures (tap targets, type sizes, contrast, icon sizes), find the SwiftUI, UIKit or AppKit API behind a piece of guidance, and adapt a design across iOS, iPadOS, macOS, tvOS, visionOS and watchOS. It routes each question to the official page and reads Apple's wording there, never from memory, and checks SwiftUI code and SF Symbol names.
